Building on this insight, we propose multi-step proximal policy improvement (MPI), a plug-in refinement mechanism that composes sequential re-centered proximal steps. MPI enables controlled policy improvement beyond dataset support while retaining proximal control at each refinement. The framework accommodates multiple policy geometries and admits practical instantiations for deterministic and diagonal-Gaussian policies. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks show that small numbers of MPI refinements improve strong offline baselines, including TD3+BC, ReBRAC, and IQL, on many tasks. Focused diagnostics further distinguish re-centered refinement from fixed-objective update scheduling and characterize limitations under critic error.

</details>


### [99] [Pushing the (Decision) Boundaries: Dynamically Calibrating Differentially Private Noise to Explainability in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03851)
*Michael Khavkin,Kichang Lee,Jaeho Jin,JeongGil Ko,Eran Toch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了XCal-FL，一种用于跨孤岛联邦学习图像分类的闭环可解释性驱动的本地训练算法，通过三种互补信号（预测logit变化、反事实边界、显著性集中度）动态校准差分隐私噪声，在保持形式化DP保证的同时，显著提升了全局模型的预测性能与解释保真度，并揭示了可解释性是区别于效用的独立隐私权衡维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带有差分隐私的联邦学习虽然被广泛采用以保护分布式机器学习中的数据机密性，但DP噪声会扭曲学到的表示并降低解释保真度，这限制了在需要可信解释的DP-FL场景（如辅助临床诊断）中的应用。现有工作使用静态特征重要性信号来适配DP噪声，局限在事后分析层面，无法在训练过程中根据解释质量对噪声进行校准。

Method: 提出了XCal-FL闭环训练算法：从三个互补信号动态校准DP噪声——(1)预测logit变化衡量对模型置信度的因果影响；(2)反事实边界捕捉决策边界敏感性；(3)显著性集中度量化模型注意力的空间一致性；同时通过自适应隐私记账强制执行形式化DP保证。该机制在本地训练过程中实时响应解释质量反馈，形成闭环校准。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上跨越多种FL配置的实验表明：XCal-FL相比静态噪声FL，预测性能提升超过10%，解释保真度提升最高达5倍；在保真度指标上优于最先进的自适应DP方法；同时实现了更高的隐私预算效率，每单位累计隐私损失的效用和解释保真度增益更大。分析还发现预测性能与隐私损失近似线性相关，而解释保真度呈现非线性动态。

Conclusion: 可解释性是隐私权衡中一个独立于效用的维度，无法仅从预测性能推断。这一发现对于决策关键型应用中的训练策略和隐私预算分配具有重要启示。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Differential Privacy (DP) is increasingly adopted to preserve data confidentiality in distributed machine learning. However, DP noise distorts learned representations and degrades explanation fidelity, limiting differentially private FL where trustworthy explanations are required, such as assistive clinical diagnosis. Prior work adapted DP noise with static feature-importance signals, restricting explainability to post hoc analysis and precluding noise calibration to explanation quality during training. We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [100] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究注意力机制的训练动力学，提出了注意力索引模型（attention-indexed models）这一统一框架来分析多层多头的注意力架构。在高维极限下，群体损失景观可由有限维的迹序参数刻画；而在线SGD则由无限维矩阵矩层级控制，该层级可被有限截断系统指数级逼近。文章还揭示了注意力参数化本身构成一种架构隐式偏向：直接优化注意力矩阵S可能陷入无信息状态，而带状注意力（S=WW^T）能自动触发对称性破缺并在Θ(d²log d)样本内实现弱恢复；非带状注意力（S=UV^T）则展现出快慢机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但其训练动力学（特别是注意力矩阵具有高秩时的动态行为）仍缺乏深入理解。作者旨在建立一个宽泛的理论框架来统一分析不同参数化方式下注意力模型的训练行为，并揭示参数化选择如何影响学习动力学。

Method: 提出attention-indexed models框架，可表示多层多头的注意力架构。在高维极限下，通过迹序参数（trace order parameters）刻画群体损失的景观；推导在线SGD的无限维矩阵矩层级动力学，并证明其可用有限截断系统指数级逼近。通过比较直接优化注意力矩阵S、带状参数化S=WW^T和非带状参数化S=UV^T三种方式揭示架构隐式偏差。

Result: 直接优化S会陷入无信息的稳定态；带状注意力参数化诱导自动的对称性破缺机制，在Θ(d²log d)样本内实现弱恢复；非带状注意力展现出快慢机制——预激活均值在快时间尺度上演化，重叠量在慢时间尺度上演化，当快速动力学选中的状态打破初始对称性时可在Θ(d²log d)样本量级实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化的选择本身就构成了重要的隐式偏差，影响训练能否逃脱无信息状态并实现有效学习。该框架为理解注意力架构的训练动力学提供了新的理论工具，揭示了不同参数化方式（直接、带状、非带状）在对称性破缺和学习效率上的本质差异。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [101] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFFINT，一种结构可解释的深度自编码器异常检测方法。其潜在瓶颈被组织为一组软性、轴向对齐的区间隶属度集合，从原始数值数据端到端学习。模型兼具深度表示学习的性能与可检查的内部结构，在任何异常标签可见之前即可输出封闭形式的、无需标签的重要性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重构的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）虽然准确率高，但模型是"黑箱"——检测出异常样本后，无法告知实践者是哪些特征范围导致该样本被判定为异常。作者希望在不牺牲深度学习表示能力的前提下，赋予模型可检查（inspectable）的内部结构，使检测结果可以被审计和解释。

Method: DIFFINT的自编码器潜在瓶颈被结构化为一组软的、轴向对齐的区间隶属度，直接从原始数值数据端到端学习，无需离散化或二值化。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的一个人类可读的超矩形；样本被编码为它落入每个区间与其它单元相比的强度，重构误差作为异常分数。作者通过Lipschitz约束的解码器给出严格的归纳偏置证明（超支持外部点的重构误差下界），并设计了逐级可验证的抑制机制处理仅少数特征异常的情形。重要性度量以封闭形式、无需标签地计算每个(单元, 特征)对的排序。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上、与22个基线在[-1,1]归一化协议下对比，DIFFINT在两个指标上均取得最优平均排名（ROC-AUC为4.10，AUPR为4.16）。在仅用内点训练的检测器中明显领先，与最强的含污染数据检测器具有竞争力。在统计上并列的七个领先方法集群中，DIFFINT是唯一可解释的检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT证明了解释性并不需要牺牲异常检测性能：其可审计的区间结构在统计上与最先进方法并列，同时提供无需标签的特征级归因和超矩形约束的审计能力，填补了重构式异常检测中"准确但不可解释"的空白。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出持续知识更新方法仅用单一最终检查点和单一适配器秩来评估是不充分的。通过在24个月Wikidata流上对比周期性层次结构与累积回放，改变评估月份、回放LoRA秩和查询表述后，胜负关系会发生反转（如Qwen2.5-1.5B上优势5.0分变为劣势11.6分）。作者提议报告轨迹与容量扫描，在评估区域内排序稳定时才宣告稳健胜者，否则报告胜者区域和保留-稳定-成本前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识更新方法的评估常基于单一最终检查点和单一适配器秩来判定方法优劣，但这种方式可能不足以识别真正的更优工作点，方法排名可能依赖评估时刻和回放侧适配容量等条件。

Method: 固定周期性层次结构，在24个月的Wikidata流上与累积回放方法对比，同时变化三个维度：评估月份、回放LoRA秩、查询表述；在Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-1B及保留改写数据上验证排序反转现象，并提出报告轨迹+容量扫描的评估协议。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，层次结构相对秩-8回放有5.0分优势，但相对秩-72回放则变为11.6分劣势；高秩下一致性对齐端点看似平局，而时间平均回放领先9-13分。秩相关的反转在Llama-3.2-1B和保留改写数据上也复现。

Conclusion: 持续更新中的方法排序可能同时依赖于性能测量时机和基线获得的回放侧适配容量。评估应报告轨迹和容量扫描，仅在评估区域上排序稳定时才宣告稳健胜者；否则应报告胜者区域及保留-稳定-成本前沿。据此，周期性层次结构应被视为低更新成本工作点，而非质量胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [103] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RATL是一种面向多元时间序列预测的插件式残差检索与反馈修正方法。它冻结基础预测器构建检索键，将历史预测残差作为仅训练期的记忆存储，推理时在因果可用性约束下检索相似历史残差轨迹，并通过面向预测块和变量的集合感知路由器进行选择和组合，从而在不重新训练基础模型的情况下提升其预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）的理念虽吸引人，但直接复用于连续输出回归时，直接重用检索到的目标值对输出水平、数值尺度或局部动力学不同的样本不够鲁棒。此外，传统预测流程虽使用残差进行优化和误差诊断，却不保留个体历史残差作为推理时可访问的记忆。这促使研究者思考从检索目标值转向检索模型自身的历史预测误差。

Method: RATL首先冻结基础预测器（base forecaster）以构造检索键（retrieval keys），并将其历史预测残差转化为该基础模型特有的训练期记忆（train-only memory）。推理时，RATL在因果可用性约束下检索来自相似历史上下文的残差轨迹，然后使用一个集合感知路由器（set-aware router）在预测块和变量上进行选择与组合，最后通过验证集上的修正强度选择来防止过度注入残差，实现反馈修正。

Result: 实验表明，与当前上下文匹配的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息；RATL在大多数实验设置下能提升冻结的基础预测器性能。以iTransformer作为主要基础预测器并在真实基准上测试，RATL优于多种强预测基线，并展现出跨骨干网络的迁移能力。消融实验证实学习式路由能增强原始残差反馈，验证集上的修正强度选择可限制残差过度注入。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为特定于基础模型的历史预测误差，提供了一种插件式、基于残差记忆的连续输出预测中的学习反馈修正范式。该范式在不修改或重新训练基础模型的情况下，通过残差检索与路由选择实现预测性能提升。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [104] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种用于GRPO的组级回放控制原语，将回放选择分解为两个决策：Headroom按剩余学习价值对存储组排序，Drift按其与当前策略的兼容性进行门控。该方法不改变新鲜on-policy流，不引入额外的生成或训练机制。在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上，该方法优于朴素回放并匹配或超越更复杂的回放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RL的后训练在推理模型中正日益受到重复新鲜rollout生成的瓶颈限制，尤其是在智能体（agentic）场景中环境交互主导墙钟时间成本。现有回放（replay）方法通常嵌入更大的训练管线中（如与探索、经验重构或混合策略优化相结合），导致回放本身的贡献难以被单独评估。因此作者希望探究一个聚焦问题：仅靠原理性的回放选择，能产生多大效果？

Method: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种面向GRPO的组级回放控制原语，将回放复用拆分为两个独立决策：（1）Headroom：根据存储组的剩余学习价值进行排名，识别哪些组仍有进一步学习的空间；（2）Drift：通过当前策略与存储组的兼容性进行门控筛选，只回放与当前策略不过度漂移的组。新鲜on-policy数据流保持不变，不添加任何辅助生成或训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上，该单一干预策略在Avg Mean@32上优于朴素回放，并能匹配或超越更广泛/更复杂的回放方法。在Agentic Search中（此处环境交互主导成本），该方法在不降低质量的同时显著减少了墙钟时间。

Conclusion: 回放的选择策略本身可以作为一个独立且有效的干预手段来缓解RL后训练中的生成瓶颈。Headroom与Drift这两组简单而原理化的决策标准，在多个领域基准上都表现出与更复杂的回放框架相当甚至更优的性能，且在环境交互成本高的场景中能显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [105] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VestigeKV提出了一种面向NoPE MLA长上下文模型（Kimi Linear）的无训练KV缓存压缩方法。它利用缓存中携带的64维解耦分支（RoPE的残余，被NoPE训练重定向为显著性通道）作为查询无关的淘汰信号，仅读取矩阵每行的11%即可对缓存分层：top-m行保留在注意力层，其余行不会被删除而是精确移动到GPU驻留的归档区域。在8k到65k上下文下，该方法在8x压缩下检索保持1.00，32x下为0.92，且与全行选择无差距。注意层在32x下仅占每token缓存8.1KB中的0.25KB。该方法仅适用于NoPE模型，在RoPE MLA上会崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命KV缓存在查询出现之前就必须被压缩，而基于观测注意力（如H2O、SnapKV）的选择方法在此时失效（在NoPE MLA模型上针检索仅为0.00-0.33），因为token的重要性尚未被观测到。需要一个无需未来查询即可工作的查询无关压缩信号。

Method: VestigeKV利用Kimi Linear每个token缓存中已经携带的64维解耦分支作为查询无关的淘汰信号——这是RoPE的残留，NoPE训练将其重新用于显著性通道。算法仅读取每行11%数据，将缓存分成两层：top-m行留在注意力层，其余行精确（永不被删除）移动到GPU驻留的归档区，可通过认证触发器逐步访问。该方法无需训练、量化，也不改动权重或内核。若需回收VRAM，可提供主机卸载作为变体。

Result: 在8k至65k上下文范围内，8x压缩下检索准确率保持1.00，32x下为0.92，与全行选择的差距为零。32x压缩下注意力层仅占Kimi Linear每token 8.1KB缓存中的0.25KB，归档区保持位精确且GPU驻留。标准配置的召回层在128x下保持1.00。对比实验显示：同样的操作在RoPE MLA上崩溃至0.08（普通淘汰为0.42）。查询无关的显著性本身只存在于无旋转的情况（top-1目标覆盖token的2.3-6.7%，对比RoPE下的10.2-46.8%），且RoPE下查询通用的精确合并被证明不可能。

Conclusion: VestigeKV证明NoPE模型的解耦分支可被重用为高效的查询无关压缩信号，在极大压缩率下仍保持无损检索并实现零可测开销。该方法的适用性严格限于NoPE模型：其原理在RoPE模型上不可行，甚至存在理论不可行性证明。作者对于是否推广到同样采用NoPE Gated-MLA架构的Kimi K3持谨慎态度，仅在有匹配缓存布局时可能适用，但不做超出所测模型的声明。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [106] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输出空间统计重分布的方法，用于在分类系统标签移除后无需重新训练或特征空间调整即可近似重训模型的后移除置信向量，兼顾计算效率与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类系统中标签移除频繁发生（如动态演化的分类体系），现有方法（重训练法和特征空间调整法）依赖原始数据访问、计算与存储成本高、结果不一致、可扩展性差且损害模型效用，亟需一种更轻量、隐私友好的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种新型方法，通过在输出空间中进行统计重分布，近似模拟重训练模型在类别移除后的置信度向量。该方法可作为模块化的输出过滤器直接应用，无需特征空间调整或损失函数收敛，仅依赖现有标签和先验输出置信度。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个分类任务上与完整重训练相比表现出有竞争力的性能，同时在计算效率和隐私保护方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的输出空间统计重分布方法有效克服了现有标签移除技术的局限性，在无需重训的前提下达成近似重训练效果，兼顾模型效用、可扩展性与数据隐私。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [107] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍RobustSeiz，一个开源、模型无关的癫痫发作检测器鲁棒性评估框架，提供标准化的协议在受控的临床相关分布偏移（环境干扰、噪声、对抗输入）下对检测器进行压力测试，并与Docker化GPU流程、实验注册表和完整评估/研究子集模式集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作检测器在留出EEG数据上表现良好，但在真实世界的采集变异性、伪影和对抗输入下可能失效。现有评估往往局限于干净数据的准确性，缺乏在部署前对检测器在受控临床扰动下进行标准化、可重复的压力测试框架。

Method: 将四个公开头皮EEG语料库（CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena、SeizeIT1）标准化为BIDS-EEG树结构；在留出分割上评估主体无关检测器；在预定义超参数网格上扫描环境、噪声和对抗变换；报告样本级和事件级敏感度、精密度、F1、每24小时假阳性率、Lead/Lag发作时间以及蒙特卡洛dropout预测一致性；提供Docker化GPU流水线、实验注册表以及全评估和研究子集两种模式。

Result: 使用当代癫痫检测器在TUSZ上演示了完整变换网格上的框架；AWGN分析表明扰动严重程度如何改变检测质量、发作时间判定和预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz为评估癫痫检测器在现实临床压力环境下的鲁棒性提供了共享基准标准，将部署前评估从单纯的干净数据准确性扩展到更全面的鲁棒性考量。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uno，一种扩散增强LLM新范式，将AR模型分布与并行扩散采样相结合，实现无损加速和推理时扩展，无需额外草稿模型，显著提升生成吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs依赖自回归（AR）逐token顺序生成，导致推理速度成为瓶颈。现有方案如投机解码需要额外草稿模型，而扩散LLM (d-LLMs) 会牺牲底层AR模型质量。作者旨在克服AR顺序生成的限制，同时保持AR模型分布质量，实现高效的并行多token生成。

Method: 提出扩散增强LLM（diffusion-augmented LLMs），将参数解耦为两组：AR权重（用标准NTP目标训练）和轻量扩散权重（通过简单Diffusion Distillation阶段学习多token并行生成）。同时设计Psi-Spec采样器族，实现固定上下文长度下的无损加速和推理时扩展。

Result: Uno在每批次大小下的吞吐量均超越领先投机解码方法，相比基础AR模型实现高达3倍加速（包括设备支持的最大批次）。8B Uno模型在agentic工具使用、编码和长上下文推理的所有评估基准上均优于领先的开源26B DiffusionGemma和专有Mercury 2模型。

Conclusion: Uno可从零训练或通过增强现有开源权重AR LLM构建，兼具AR模型质量与扩散并行生成效率。相比投机解码无需草稿模型，相比d-LLMs不牺牲底层AR质量，为LLM推理加速提供了新的高效路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [109] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种位置不变的极值分位处理效应（QTE）估计方法，通过将位置不变的Fraga极值指数估计器应用于因果推断场景（结合逆倾向得分加权），并采用基于差分的外推方案来消除位置参数的影响，从而解决了现有极值QTE估计器在共同位置平移下不满足不变性的问题。作者建立了估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，并提供了有效的方差估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极值分位处理效应在极端分位水平下的估计对重尾分布的结果变量至关重要，但现有的极值QTE估计器依赖于外推与因果极值指数（EVI）估计的结合，该估计器在潜在结果分布的共同位置平移下不具不变性，而总体QTE却具有这种不变性。

Method: 本文采用两步策略：（1）将位置不变的Fraga极值指数估计器通过逆倾向得分加权（IPSW）适配到因果设置中；（2）用基于差分的外推方案替代原始外推公式，在取分位差时位置参数得以抵消，从而使QTE估计量具有位置不变性。作者证明了所提估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，并提供了相合的方差估计量。

Result: 模拟研究表明所提出的方法具有位置不变性、对阈值的稳定性，以及良好的置信区间覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了极值分位处理效应估计中的位置不变性问题，所提出的估计器在理论性质和有限样本表现上均表现良好，能够用于渐近有效的统计推断。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [110] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF，一种样本高效的奖励模型不确定度追踪方法，将主动偏好学习建模为顺序贝叶斯滤波问题，在低维参数子空间内通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行序列推断，避免了全神经网络参数空间上计算昂贵的后验推断，从而高效地计算主动学习采集函数。实验表明该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF虽强大但样本效率低下，主动学习对于合成信息丰富的偏好查询至关重要。然而，大型神经网络奖励模型的有效不确定度量化（这是主动学习所需的核心条件）仍然是一个关键挑战。

Method: 将主动偏好学习框架化为顺序贝叶斯滤波问题；在低维参数子空间内通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器执行序列推断，持续更新奖励模型后验；利用可扩展的神经网络参数采样高效计算主动奖励学习的采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，PreferenceEKF在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面均优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，且学到的奖励模型带来了具有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了可扩展贝叶斯方法在RLHF中基于偏好的奖励建模方面的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [111] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用因果最优传输（Causal Optimal Transport）定义近似的损失函数，以替代传统的评分函数，用于条件生成模型中对扩散过程的引导控制，即使在奇异扩散系数或条件密度不存在/不光滑的情况下也能成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型高度依赖评分函数（score functions）进行训练引导，但当扩散过程的扩散系数奇异、且底层条件密度不存在或不光滑时，传统的评分函数方法失效或缺乏理论保证。

Method: 利用因果最优传输及其通过条件扩散过程的可预测表示性质（Üstünel意义下的鞅问题适定）的表征，定义近似损失函数，从而在极弱假设下识别最小熵控制（即引导项）。

Result: 在最小假设条件下，成功定义了可用于引导扩散生成模型的近似损失函数，该函数通过因果最优传输识别出最小熵控制。

Conclusion: 因果最优传输为处理奇异扩散系数和缺失/非光滑条件密度情况下的条件扩散生成模型提供了一条严谨的理论路径。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [112] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Direct-P方法，解决Blackwell FP4张量核心无法自动加速注意力计算的问题（因softmax转换和片上依赖成为瓶颈）。通过非因果推理的Direct-P路径和因果路径（将前向量化直接传入反向传播），在GB200上非因果前向吞吐最高达BF16的2.13倍，单GPU 80亿参数完整更新最高加速1.14倍；分布式训练中，所有MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散，而所提方法保留FP8概率与值可保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell的4位浮点（FP4）张量核心虽然具备计算优势，但当矩阵乘积缩小后，softmax转换和芯片上依赖关系成为主导开销，导致FP4张量核心并不能自动加速注意力计算。作者希望设计新的注意力前向与反向传播路径，使概率计算直接以低精度格式运行，从而真正利用FP4硬件的加速能力。

Method: 提出两种路径：(1) Direct-P路径用于非因果推理，将注意力分数直接映射为FP4概率，避免中间格式转换开销；(2) 因果路径在反向传播中直接复用前向量化的查询与键来重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数计算。分布式训练中保留FP8概率与值而非降为MXFP4。

Result: 在NVIDIA GB200上，Direct-P的非因果前向吞吐最高达BF16的2.13倍；因果路径以8位浮点梯度操作数加速完整单GPU 80亿参数训练更新，最高提速1.14倍。测试中所有MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散，匹配的分布式训练需保留FP8精度才能收敛。

Conclusion: Direct-P有效克服了FP4张量核心在注意力计算中的瓶颈，通过将低精度量化直接整合到forward/backward路径中实现显著加速；同时揭示了概率和值部分若过度降低到MXFP4精度会导致训练发散，FP8是该场景下的必要精度选择。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [113] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非耦合学习算法HOOD（高阶乐观带折扣），用于任意N人标准型博弈，每个玩家最多K个动作，可在整个博弈时段上保证每个玩家的个体后悔度为O(N³log²K)，这是迄今为止达到常数级后悔度（相对于博弈次数T）的最佳结果之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般博弈中实现常数级个体后悔度（即不随博弈次数增长）是一个长期以来的挑战。此前的方法（如OptFTRL）在一般博弈中会因诱导博弈序列的大幅振荡而无法实现常数后悔度。作者旨在通过精心设计的机制消除这一关键障碍，从而实现均匀的常数后悔度保证。

Method: 提出的算法称为"高阶乐观带折扣"（HOOD），是乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的一个变体。其核心创新包括：(1) 使用折扣的(N+1)阶预测器；(2) 在博弈策略空间的适当"提升"（lifting）上应用熵正则化。这两种成分的组合设计目的是以受控方式抑制诱导博弈序列的大幅振荡，从而消除先前方法在一般博弈中实现常数后悔度的关键障碍。

Result: 对于任意N人标准型博弈（每玩家至多K个动作），HOOD算法保证每个玩家的个体后悔度为O(N³log²K)，且该保证在博弈时段上均匀成立（即不随时间T增长）。作者的界O(N³log²K)显著优于并发独立工作中Liu、Farina和Ozdaglar得到的O(N²¹log⁴K)界。

Conclusion: 通过高阶乐观预测与折扣策略以及策略空间提升上的熵正则化的结合，成功实现了在一般N人博弈中的常数个体后悔度，且复杂度表现远优于同期独立工作。该工作为解决一般博弈中的高效学习问题提供了重要的理论突破。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [114] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了递归正交滤波器（RQFs）——一类生物启发的复值时间滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特例），并引入"前摄输入编码"（prospective-input coding）机制来解决深层连续时间递归网络中时间积分导致的底层信号延迟和顶层误差衰减问题。该方法在多种模型（RQFs、S5、ORGaNICs）上均优于或持平非前摄对照，并在Speech Commands和Path-X任务上取得了优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分赋予连续时间递归网络记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中会延迟自下而上的信号并衰减自上而下的误差信号。这种深度相关的梯度衰减问题阻碍了深层连续时间递归网络的训练和性能表现。作者希望探索能否通过使每一层的前摄输入变得"超前"（prospective）来解决这一失效模式。

Method: 1) 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF为带通滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。2) 提出无参数的二步更新（two-tap update）使每层的自下而上输入变得前摄，保持循环转置和平行扫描不变。3) 将该校正扩展到一般对角SSM，在截断时间梯度（即仅空间反向传播）情况下缓解深度相关的梯度衰减。4) 在RQFs、S5和ORGaNICs上使用完整BPTT和仅空间反向传播进行评估。

Result: 完整BPTT下，前摄变体在所有模型和配置中均匹配或优于非前摄对照。非残差宽度32六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上达96.09%准确率（仅31.9k参数）；宽度64六层RQF在16,384步Path-X任务上达83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs是参数高效的递归基础架构，前摄输入编码是对深层连续时间递归网络的一种输入端校正，能有效缓解深度相关的时间梯度截断误差衰减。该工作为时间递归模型的深度训练提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [115] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个低成本的开放式微型Ackermann车辆实验平台，用于端到端自动驾驶研究。平台结合物理车辆、打印城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生。作为基线，实现了命令条件行为克隆。实验表明真实车辆上横向误差为6.1 cm（接近人类演示的4.7 cm），数字孪生中增大相机视场可将误差从35.6 cm降至3.3 cm，且结合合成数据与真实演示训练的高容量策略是唯一能完成全部四条赛道闭环任务的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶研究多依赖昂贵的真实车辆或纯仿真环境，缺乏低成本、可复现且能连接仿真与真实世界的开放实验平台。作者希望填补这一空白，为端到端自动驾驶的sim-to-real研究提供一个可控、低成本、开源的测试平台。

Method: 构建了集物理微型车、打印城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生于一体的开放式实验平台。实现了命令条件行为克隆（神经网络接收车载摄像头图像和高层导航命令，输出转向与速度），并引入sim-to-real图像翻译器缩小合成数据与真实数据的外观差距。分别在物理车辆和数字孪生中评估了不同相机视场、不同模型容量及不同训练数据组合（纯合成、纯真实、混合）的影响。

Result: 真实闭环实验中，学习策略的横向误差均值为6.1 cm，接近人类演示的4.7 cm。数字孪生中，相机视场从58度扩至120度可将误差从35.6 cm降至3.3 cm。在四条赛道闭环测试中，只有使用合成数据+真实演示联合训练的高容量策略能全部完成，而紧凑基线和仅用真实数据训练的同网络只能完成较少赛道。

Conclusion: 该开放平台被验证为实用的sim-to-real研究测试平台，命令条件模仿学习可作为有效基线。平台已开源发布，支持可复现研究，为自动驾驶社区提供连接仿真与真实实验的低成本基础设施。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [116] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一般和并发随机博弈（CSG）在转移不确定性下的PAC学习框架，该框架要么返回社会福祉最优的ε-近似纳什均衡（NE），要么给出不存在精确NE的可靠证书；算法在最小可达性条件下具有多项式样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般和并发随机博弈中，在转移核未知的情况下进行强化学习面临两大核心挑战：一是NE的存在性并非总能保证，现有方法往往隐含假设NE一定存在；二是在不确定环境中需要同时解决探索与均衡计算问题。此前缺乏既能学习出ε-NE又能处理NE不存在情形的统一PAC框架。

Method: 算法维护基于数据的L1置信集覆盖转移核，求解鲁棒CSG以计算社会福祉最优的ε-NE，并使用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作对的覆盖。关键创新在于引入Nash margin刻画，使得框架能够从原理上推理均衡存在性：要么返回社会福祉值与最优值ε-接近的ε-近似NE，要么输出证明不存在精确NE的可靠证书。此外需满足可达性条件p_reach>0。

Result: 在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max² H⁴ |S|² |A| / (p_reach ε²))。基准CSG上的经验结果展示了近最优性能、对均衡存在/不存在情形的正确处理，以及样本复杂度与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在统一PAC框架下解决了CSG学习中NE存在性与学习效率的双重挑战，既提供了可证明的均衡学习保证，又为解决NE缺失这一此前被回避的根本性问题提供了原则性的判定机制，理论与实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由人工智能驱动的应用英语新教材体系，采用五层架构（知识图谱、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈协调和教师端治理），并在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了八周教学实验。相比静态数字教材，系统将单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%，证明AI驱动教材能在维持课程稳定性的同时提供个性化学习路径和实践数据追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在改变应用英语教材的形态，使其从固定纸张序列向自适应学习系统转变，后者能够诊断学习者、推荐任务并提供形成性反馈。本文旨在研究这种新型AI驱动教材的结构与应用方法。

Method: 提出了一个五层架构的AI驱动英语教材系统，包括：知识图谱层、学习者画像层、任务生成层、反馈协调层和教师端治理层。随后将原型系统应用于186名非英语专业本科生的八周教学实验，与静态数字教材进行对比评估。

Result: 相较于静态数字教材，AI驱动系统将单元完成准确率从72.4%提高到84.9%，口语任务平均分数提升10.8分，并将教师的批改时间缩短了31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在保持课程标准稳定性的同时，提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料以及可追踪的课堂数据，是一种可行且有效的教材创新方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [118] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种"受治理"的企业分析方案：语言模型仅负责理解问题意图，而确定性策略负责选择并执行预先审批的分析程序，该程序既返回结果也返回证据。在440次运行中，三种8B模型在运行时自主生成SQL并选择工具，均未能完整满足"答案+证据"合约；而由策略执行预先审批程序的方式在110/110次运行中全部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析场景中，直接让语言模型在运行时自主规划（生成SQL、选择工具）虽然灵活，但难以保证结果的可信性、可审计性和可复现性。作者希望探索一种受约束的治理框架，在保持表达能力的同时，确保分析结果符合企业治理要求（如合规性、证据可追溯性）。

Method: 提出一种"治理式"分析架构：(1) 语言模型仅做意图理解；(2) 确定性策略负责在预先审批的分析程序库中选取合适的程序并执行；(3) 分析程序基于关系运算（含聚合、比较、窗口、排序、相似度）构建，在限定的分析类别内具备表达力；(4) 固定的语义、策略、数据和执行规则确保结果可重放。实验中对比了三种8B模型在运行时自主生成SQL/选择工具的方案与策略执行方案的性能。

Result: 在440次运行中，三种8B模型自主规划（共330次运行时规划场景）没有一种能在所有测试数据集上完整满足"答案+证据"合约；而策略执行预先审批程序的方式在110/110次运行中全部成功匹配合约。

Conclusion: 在配置特定的实验条件下，治理式的策略执行方案在满足"答案+证据"合约方面显著优于运行时自主规划方案。但作者强调这是一个配置相关的结论，并不意味着在其他设计下运行时智能体无法成功。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [119] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Macro Commit (SMC)，一种面向工具使用型LLM智能体的运行时机制。SMC采用双层级智能体架构：权威大模型决定正式轨迹，快速投机草稿模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测并执行未来动作链。SMC从训练轨迹中挖掘重复的多动作骨架存入宏库，运行时将草稿模型预测的动作链与宏进行匹配。当权威模型的下一个工具调用与草稿的第一个动作匹配时，SMC将预执行的草稿步骤及其观察结果提交到正式轨迹，从而大幅减少串行动作-观察轮次的等待延迟。实验表明，SMC在τ²-Bench Telecom子集和AppWorld上分别比串行执行降低18.59%和44.9%的延迟，同时基本保持或略微降低任务完成准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型LLM智能体在实际运行中不仅需要模型推理时间，还涉及大量串行的动作-观察轮次，每次工具调用、环境转换和观察都可能延迟后续决策。现有的单步投机执行(Speculative Actions)虽然能预测下一步动作，但无法充分利用多步行动中被重复执行的模式，导致延迟仍未被充分降低。因此需要一种能重用多步投机执行的机制来进一步提升智能体运行效率。

Method: SMC采用双层级智能体架构：(1)权威actor模型产生正式轨迹，确保决策质量；(2)更快的投机drafter模型持续预测未来动作链，并在隔离的环境快照上预先执行。SMC从训练轨迹中挖掘重复出现的多动作骨架(skeletons)，存入宏库(macro library)，用于运行时匹配drafter预测的动作链。当actor的下一个工具调用与drafter预测的第一个动作一致时，SMC将剩余的预执行草稿步骤及其观察结果一次性提交到正式轨迹中，实现多步投机提交。

Result: 以Qwen3.5-27B INT4为权威actor模型、Qwen3.5-4B为投机drafter模型，SMC在τ²-Bench Telecom子集上与顺序执行准确率持平，延迟相比Speculative Actions (SA)基线降低10.23%，相比顺序执行降低18.59%。在AppWorld上，SMC相比SA基线减少7.7%墙钟时间，相比顺序执行减少44.9%，任务完成率仅有小幅下降。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的方式，将多步投机执行从单步扩展到多步复用，有效降低了智能体推理过程中的串行延迟，而无需牺牲整体决策准确性。该工作表明宏库驱动的多动作骨架匹配是一种可行且高效的工具型智能体加速方案。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [120] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFence，一种依赖范围限定的动作验证协议，用于解决分布式LLM智能体团队中的"过期计划执行"问题——即状态虽新鲜但授权动作的计划已失效的情况。在30个受控实时工作流中，PlanFence相比纯新鲜度执行者避免了所有无效动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM智能体团队即使读取了最新的共享事实，仍可能基于过时的计划行动。问题根源在于：状态新鲜度（state freshness）并不等同于计划有效性（plan validity）——即状态虽然是最新同步的，但计划所依赖的推理前提可能已被后续提交或其他智能体的操作所推翻。这种"过期计划执行"（stale-plan execution）会导致无效或错误动作的发生。

Method: PlanFence是一种依赖范围限定的动作验证协议：计划会标注其所引用的确切公开记录（plans cite the exact public records they used），执行器仅验证那些可能影响待执行外部动作的记录，验证不完整时执行器会重新规划一次或阻塞。该协议将验证范围从全局状态收窄到与当前动作相关的依赖子集。

Result: 在30个含计划修订的受控实时工作流中：纯新鲜度执行者在每个任务中都基于过期计划行动，而PlanFence在所有任务中均无无效动作地完成。受控重放实验揭示两个条件边界：低变更率（churn）下，主动同步的协调停滞更低；高变更率下，PlanFence避免了重复的更新路径协调；共享键空间增大时，PlanFence避免验证无关状态。

Conclusion: PlanFence提供了受控条件下的安全性和系统成本优势，但作者明确界定这不是通用任务准确率的提升。该工作表明依赖作用域跟踪是解决多智能体计划失效问题的关键方向，并揭示了不同同步策略在不同条件下存在权衡。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [121] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于提示工程的框架，用于个性化基于通用大语言模型（LLM/RAG）的AI助教（如Jill Watson），通过六个学习者维度（自我评估、抽象偏好、详细程度偏好、感知取向、信息处理风格和理解水平）组合成96种学习档案，并结合布鲁姆分类法分析学生查询的认知复杂性，将学习者属性和认知评估编码为结构化提示词条件化LLM（无需模型重训练），实现跨学科和课程的个性化教育支持。实验采用NLP指标和五项参与者的人体研究进行评估，结果显示不同个性化条件下响应风格和结构存在感知差异，且统计检验识别出与可测量响应变化相关联的学习者属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的AI教学助手虽能提供可扩展的教育支持，但缺乏个性化能力，无法根据学习者的个体差异调整教学响应。现有通用LLM/RAG系统难以针对不同学科、课程和学习者特征提供定制化辅助，需要一种无需模型重训练即可实现个性化的通用框架。

Method: 提出基于提示工程（prompt engineering）的个性化框架，定义六个学习者维度（自我评估、抽象偏好、详细程度偏好、感知取向、信息处理风格、理解水平）以构建96种学习者画像；使用布鲁姆分类法在交互层面估计学生查询的认知复杂度；将学习者特征与认知评估编码为结构化提示词以条件化LLM（如Jill Watson），无需模型重训练；通过NLP指标和包含5名参与者的人体实验评估框架效果。

Result: 实验表明，不同个性化条件下的响应在风格与结构上存在可感知的差异；统计分析识别出与可测量响应变化显著相关的特定学习者属性，为提示驱动的个性化方法提供了初步实证证据。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步证据表明，基于提示的个性化能够使LLM驱动的教育代理展现适应性行为，为无需重新训练模型即可实现个性化疗愈了这一通用大语言模型教育应用的关键空白，但也指出需要更大规模研究进一步验证其教育效果。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [122] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事俘获"（narrative captivity）这一新失效模式：在多人际冲突的叙事咨询场景中，大语言模型面对多轮、无对抗的一方自辩式叙述时，会将其当作完整信息并倾向于叙述者立场，缺乏寻求缺失视角的独立性。基于5,078个跨六类道德维度的冲突场景基准测试，17个LLM中该现象普遍存在，多轮叙事下的最终判断相对单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点；阶段分析表明偏好优化是主要贡献因素，四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM道德咨询的研究大多基于单轮判断或施加压力的反驳场景，与真实世界多轮道德咨询的交互方式不符。真实道德冲突对话常呈现单方自我合理化叙述并持续多轮，造成信息不对称。此前研究未明确：在没有明确对立观点的情况下，仅靠叙述本身是否足以在多轮道德咨询中改变模型判断。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 引入"叙事俘获"概念，构建包含5,078个人际冲突场景的基准测试集，覆盖六个道德维度。通过多轮叙述设置对比单轮基线的模型端态判断偏移量来量化该失效模式；对17个LLM进行测试，并进行阶段级分析（探索偏好优化等训练阶段的贡献），同时评估四种推理时缓解策略的效果。

Result: 叙事俘获在17个LLM中广泛存在：多轮叙述下的端态判断相对匹配的单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析识别出偏好优化是主要贡献因素，而四种推理时策略仅能提供部分缓解。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一个实际高风险场景中的系统性模型弱点，表明仅通过推理时方法难以根治，需在训练范式上加以改进，以促使LLM在真实咨询中保持独立判断。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [123] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dude，首个用于论文-代码不一致性检测的双检测多智能体系统。通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制，解决了多智能体系统在跨语言粒度不对称条件下产生的过度解释和过度报告问题，在真实数据集上相比基线方法将召回率提升最高22.8%，F1分数提升最高18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学术论文提交数量的大幅增长，人工审查已无法满足需求，而现有基于单一智能体的大语言模型范式受限于上下文容量限制且只进行单向不一致性检测，导致在检测不一致性时召回率表现不佳。

Method: 提出Dude双检测多智能体系统。核心设计包括：(1) 粒度对齐协商机制，解决论文语言与代码语言之间粒度不对称所引发的过度解释和过度报告问题；(2) 两阶段显著性过滤机制，有效防止智能体错误报告不一致性。

Result: 在真实世界论文-代码不一致性数据集上，Dude相比基线方法将召回率提升了最高22.8%，F1分数提升了最高18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude通过多智能体协同架构配合粒度对齐协商与显著性过滤机制，显著提升了论文-代码不一致性检测的准确性和召回率，验证了该方法在处理跨语言粒度不对称问题上的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [124] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval)，一个用于评估全双工语音智能体在实时口语交互中隐式指令遵循能力的基准，包含1,038个测试用例和八种助手角色，并系统评估了六种实时语音系统在不同指令协议下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多通过显式轮次管理指令测试语音智能体的行为，而实际部署的智能体通常通过角色或人设配置来推断适当的对话行为。这种显式与隐式之间的差距未被现有基准覆盖，因此需要专门评估隐式指令遵循能力的基准。

Method: 构建包含1,038个测试用例、覆盖八种不同助手角色的DSB-IFEval基准；设计五种指令遵循条件协议（默认行为、显式行为指令、人设隐含行为、人设-规则组合条件、指令冲突）；采用确定性指令遵循分数(IAS)衡量实时轮次管理，使用LLM评判的人设一致分数(PAS)衡量内容一致性；在六个实时语音系统上评估。

Result: 发现架构相关的权衡：全双工模型（F-Actor和PersonaPlex）对行为指令是显式给出还是需从人设推断更敏感，在纯人设条件下遵循度分别下降9.7%和4.5%；GPT-Realtime、MiniCPM-o和Fun-Audio-Chat强烈遵循人设一致内容，但轮次行为在显式和纯人设指令之间不具适应性；即使系统能可靠遵循连贯指令其指定人设，在安全冲突场景下仍难以覆盖这些指令。

Conclusion: 推理角色隐含的行为、在合适的对话时机执行该行为以及解决相互竞争的指令，对全双工语音智能体而言仍是三个截然不同的挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [125] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONFLICTGUI基准和CONFLICTGUARD框架，用于解决GUI代理在指令冲突场景下的过度执行（overcompliance）问题——即面对不可执行的冲突指令仍盲目执行的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI代理被广泛用于执行自然语言指令，但真实用户可能因无恶意失误发出不可行的指令。可靠的代理不仅应知道如何行动，还应知道何时不行动。现有代理在可行任务上表现良好，但在冲突指令下存在严重的执行偏向性过度服从，缺乏冲突感知的终止能力。

Method: 提出CONFLICTGUI基准，涵盖指令内部冲突与指令-界面上下文冲突两类场景。提出CONFLICTGUARD框架，包含两个耦合组件：(1) 可行性验证协议——引导代理在执行前评估指令逻辑和GUI侧证据；(2) 条件动作调制机制——将代理从过度服从的执行行为转向终止导向行为。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的代理上进行的实验表明，CONFLICTGUARD显著提升了冲突任务的平均成功率，同时保持了正常GUI任务的性能不受影响。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预能够大幅提升GUI代理识别不当执行场景并克制不必要行动的能力，为构建更可靠的GUI代理提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond "Made with AI": Visualizing Provenance Density to Mitigate the Transparency Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03460)
*Qing Zhang,Yifei Huang,Juyoung Lee,Thad Starner,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"流畅性陷阱"（Fluency Trap）问题——用户因AI生成文本流畅而过度信任其幻觉内容，同时贬低被标记为AI撰写的真实内容。作者提出"证据密度"（Provenance Density）界面来可视化文本中可验证主张的密度，用户研究显示该界面显著提升用户区分真伪的能力，而技术审计发现仅靠检索密度不足，一致性否决（Consistency Veto）信号在动态查询中携带主要判别信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI让流畅文本变得廉价，用户无法再以语言流畅度作为判断真实性的代理指标。现有的"AI制作"二元标签只披露作者身份，无法展示内容背后的证据支撑，无法帮助用户辨别真伪。

Method: 提出Provenance Density证据可视化界面，展示文本中已验证主张的密度；通过81名参与者的用户研究比较理想化界面与无信号条件；用200个样本进行技术审计，评估检索密度和一致性否决等信号在动态查询中的判别能力。

Result: 理想化的Provenance Density界面产生了较大的真伪辨别差距（+4.15分，效应量d=1.82），而无信号组参与者未能表现出可检测的辨别能力。技术审计发现检索密度单独不足，一致性否决信号在动态查询中承担了主要判别作用。

Conclusion: 当AI生成内容与人类写作难以区分时，有效的透明度机制应从作者身份披露转向证据可视化，即通过展示内容背后可验证的证据来帮助用户建立信任和辨别真伪。

Abstract: As generative AI makes polished prose cheap to produce, users can no longer rely on fluency as a proxy for truth. We call this failure mode the Fluency Trap: users trust fluent hallucinations while also discounting accurate content once it is disclosed as AI-generated. Binary ``Made with AI'' labels respond with authorship disclosure, but they do not show what supports a claim. We propose Provenance Density, an evidence-visualization interface that shows the density of verified claims in a text. In a user study with 81 participants, an idealized Provenance Density interface produced a large discernment gap between truth and fabrication ($+4.15$ points, $d=1.82$), whereas participants given no signal showed no detectable discrimination. A technical audit with 200 samples shows that retrieval density alone is insufficient; unexpectedly, the Consistency Veto carries most of the discriminative signal on dynamic queries. As AI-generated content becomes indistinguishable from human writing, effective transparency must move from authorship disclosure toward evidence visualization.

</details>


### [127] [AutoGraphForge: Towards Automated Graph Theory Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03478)
*Ján Pastorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AutoGraphForge，一个自动化的图论猜想-反驳-形式化-证明流水线系统。该系统通过反例引导的迭代轮次生成猜想，采用559条经典关系的线性规划新颖性过滤器并针对约34.8万图的数据集进行测试，最终产生6,522条存活猜想（包括人工证明的二分图与正则图中歼灭数与边覆盖数的非平凡关系），随后利用Lean 4以及两个神经证明器进行形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图论猜想通常依赖人工洞察，已有系统（如Graffiti）可生成猜想但缺乏形式化验证和自动证明能力。作者旨在构建一个端到端自动化流水线，覆盖猜想生成、反例检查、新颖性筛选、形式化翻译到内核验证的全流程，缩小自动推理与数学发现之间的鸿沟，并利用大规模图数据集与HPC算力提升发现质量。

Method: 流水线由多个阶段组成：(1) 猜想起源：Graffiti3生成器基于小型快照表T提出猜想，表通过反例逐步扩充；(2) 新颖性过滤：559条经典/民俗关系在传递闭包与线性恒等替换下封闭，通过线性规划判断候选是否已被已知结果蕴含；(3) 数据集验证：对包含约348,000个图的库（House of Graphs导出、9顶点以下连通图穷举普查、多种极值族与随机模型）进行测试；(4) 反例搜索：用主动搜索算法攻击存活的候选；(5) 形式化与证明：将存活猜想翻译为Lean 4语句骨架，集成DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B与OProver-32B两个神经证明器，所有证明由内核针对固定mathlib4和自定义不变量前导做验证。

Result: 在HPC集群上运行多轮后，流水线产出6,522条通过了反例数据集、新颖性过滤和所有主动搜索的猜想。其中一些非平凡结果（如二分图和正则图中歼灭数与边覆盖数的关系）被人工证明。形式化阶段端到端实现并通过初步健全性检查，当前完整流水线正在集群上运行。

Conclusion: AutoGraphForge展示了将猜想生成、大规模反例测试、新颖性过滤和Lean 4内核验证结合的可行架构。系统已能产出经人工可证明的非平凡图论关系，并实现了自动证明阶段。该工作代表从纯猜想生成向"可验证的自动数学发现"转变的重要一步。

Abstract: We report on our ongoing project to develop a computational pipeline, AutoGraphForge, for an automated graph-theoretic conjecturing-refuting-formalizing-proving system. Conjecture generation is counterexample-guided and runs in rounds: a Graffiti3 generator proposes conjectures over a small, evolving snapshot table $T$ (initially a few hundred graphs with their computed invariants) that grows only by counterexamples to its own conjectures. A novelty filter of $559$ classical and folklore relations, closed under transitive composition and linear identity substitution, decides via a linear program whether a candidate is already implied by known results. Surviving candidates are tested against a dataset of about $348,000$ graphs, unioning the complete House of Graphs invariant export, the exhaustive census of all connected graphs on at most nine vertices, several extremal families (strongly regular, minimal Ramsey, Cayley, cages, barbells, lollipops, spiders), and random models. Counterexample-search algorithms then attack the remainder. Run for several rounds on an HPC cluster, the loop yields $6,522$ conjectures that survived the refutation dataset, the novelty filter and every active-search run -- among them nontrivial relations between the annihilation number and the edge-cover number for bipartite and regular graphs, which we prove by hand. A subsequent formalization and proving stage deterministically translates each surviving conjecture into a Lean 4 statement skeleton; every candidate proof is kernel-verified against a pinned mathlib4 and our custom invariant preamble. This stage integrates two neural provers -- DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B (served with vLLM) and the Lean-specialised OProver-32B -- behind the independent kernel check. It is implemented end-to-end and passes initial sanity checks, with the full pipeline currently running on the cluster.

</details>


### [128] [DRACO: Fine-Grained Credit Assignment with Dynamic Rubrics for Long-Horizon Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04094)
*Shubham Gandhi,Saurabh Goyal,Kiran Kate,Yara Rizk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRACO是一种用于无真值信号（outcome-blind）的长程智能体任务中的强化学习方法。它通过动态生成多准则评分rubric、在完整轨迹上打分，并将稀疏的轨迹级奖励以闭式方式再分配为逐步骤的优势值，从而在GRPO框架下实现密集化的信用分配。在AppWorld和Tau-Bench上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励的强化学习在具备程序化检查器的任务上表现良好，但大多数长程智能体领域没有这样的检查器。传统多准则rubric虽然能够提供奖励，但每个轨迹只产生一个标量分数，在跨越数十步的任务中信号过于稀疏，难以实现有效的信用分配。

Method: DRACO在训练过程中动态生成rubric以追踪策略能力的演变；每个完成的轨迹对rubric评分一次；然后通过闭式公式将该评判结果重新分配至负责相应rubric的步骤上，在GRPO中产生差异化的逐步骤优势值。该方法不引入任何额外训练的归因模块。

Result: 在AppWorld上，DRACO较基础模型提升15.9分，较使用稀疏真值奖励训练的GRPO提升5.3分，且自身不使用任何验证器。在域外Tau-Bench上，即使没有前沿评判模型，也较基础模型提升5.3分，同时超越真值奖励训练和其他rubric训练设置。

Conclusion: DRACO证明了在没有真值验证器的长程智能体任务中，通过动态rubric生成加上闭式逐步骤奖励重分配，可以有效实现细粒度的信用分配，取得优于稀疏真值奖励训练的强化学习效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards works well when a task has a programmatic checker, but most long-horizon agent domains have none. We work in the outcome-blind setting, where ground-truth success signals are not available. Multi-criteria rubrics are a popular way to supply such a reward; they are scored once per trajectory, but a single scalar is a poor signal across tens of steps. We propose DRACO: Distributing Rubric-based Advantage for Credit Optimization. It generates rubrics dynamically during training to track the policy's evolving capability, scores those rubrics once per completed trajectory, and redistributes that judgment over the steps responsible for annotated rubrics to produce differentiated per-step advantages in GRPO. The redistribution is closed-form and does not introduce any trained attribution module. On AppWorld, DRACO gains 15.9 points over the base model and 5.3 points over GRPO trained with a sparse ground-truth reward, despite not using any verifiers itself. On out-of-domain Tau-Bench, it gains 5.3 points over the base model even without a frontier judge, beating both ground-truth-reward training and other rubric-based training settings. The code for DRACO is available at https://github.com/IBM/draco.

</details>


### [129] [Making Every Tool Call Count: Necessary Tool-Evidence Path Rewards for Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03493)
*Xingming Long,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Hanqi Feng,Shaojie Zhang,Barnabas Poczos,Chao Jiang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NTEP（必要工具-证据路径）标注方案及NTEP-R奖励机制，用于细粒度监督agentic视觉语言模型（VLM）的工具调用过程，确保每次工具调用都推进推理、收集必要的证据。在七个图像基准上，8B参数的NTEP-8B显著提升了搜索导向准确率和工具使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic VLM的训练范式仅基于最终答案正确性来评估工具使用，导致证据获取与利用缺乏充分监督，引发两个关键问题：(i)模型频繁发出冗余或偏离目标的工具调用，未能收集必要证据；(ii)即使调用了合适工具，模型也常无法从观测结果中提取所需信息。

Method: 1) 提出NTEP标注方案，为每个查询显式指定必要的外部证据及对应的工具调用；2) 提出NTEP-R奖励机制，从两方面监督模型：奖励模型将调用前意图与必要证据寻求目标对齐，并确保调用后从观测中总结的信息与必要证据一致；3) 引入非重复目标正则化器，惩罚重复满足已获NTEP目标的冗余调用。

Result: 在七个图像相关基准上的广泛评估表明，8B参数的NTEP-8B在统一三工具框架中显著提升了搜索导向准确率和工具使用效率。

Conclusion: 细粒度的工具-证据路径监督对于训练鲁棒的agentic VLM至关重要，结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) can directly answer many image-grounded questions, yet they often struggle with complex queries requiring fine-grained visual details or external knowledge. To acquire this missing evidence, agentic VLMs invoke tools such as image cropping, image search, and text search. However, existing training paradigms primarily evaluate tool-use based on final answer correctness, leaving evidence acquisition and utilization insufficiently supervised. This leads to two critical shortcomings: (i) models frequently issue redundant or off-target tool calls that fail to gather necessary evidence, and (ii) even when appropriate tools are called, models often fail to extract the necessary information from the resulting observations. To address these limitations, we introduce the NTEP (Necessary Tool-Evidence Path), a novel annotation scheme that explicitly specifies the essential external evidence and corresponding tool calls for each query. Building upon this, we propose NTEP-R (NTEP Reward), a supervision mechanism ensuring that each tool invocation strictly advances the reasoning process toward the final solution. Specifically, our approach rewards the agent for aligning its pre-call intent with a necessary evidence-seeking goal, and for ensuring the information summarized from the post-call observation aligns with the necessary evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a non-repeated-goal regularizer to penalize redundant calls that revisit satisfied NTEP goals. Extensive evaluations on seven image-grounded benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter instantiation, NTEP-8B, significantly improves both search-oriented accuracy and tool-use efficiency within a unified three-tool framework. These results highlight the critical value of fine-grained tool-evidence path supervision for training robust agentic VLMs.

</details>


### [130] [GrowPage: On-Demand KV Budgeting for Efficient LLM Reasoning Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03494)
*Qiankun Ma,Yanjiang Zhou,Zinan Xiong,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Yang Xiang,Ziyao Zhang,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GrowPage是一个面向长输出推理场景的按需KV缓存预算框架，通过双时间尺度查询摘要动态估计注意力需求，在容量边界处自适应地压缩KV状态或获取额外物理页，从而在分页注意力抽象下实现更好的性能-吞吐量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使KV缓存成为LLM服务中的关键内存瓶颈。现有KV压缩方法通常依赖预定义的每请求预算，仅调整保留哪些KV状态，导致总容量在整个解码过程中固定不变。然而推理工作负载存在显著的需求变化：不同请求需要不同的KV容量，且单个请求的注意力需求在生成过程中也会演变。这种静态预算策略无法适应需求的动态变化。

Method: GrowPage将KV容量视为运行时资源，而非预先固定的限制。具体做法：(1) 维护轻量级双时间尺度查询摘要，分别捕捉短期和长期注意力行为；(2) 利用这些摘要的相对注意力工作集来估计需求演变趋势；(3) 在每个容量边界处，若当前分配内可容纳则压缩KV状态，否则获取额外物理页以应对更广泛的注意力需求；(4) 与PagedAttention的页级内存抽象集成，保持连续批处理和前缀缓存的兼容性。

Result: 在多个模型上的推理基准实验中，GrowPage相比现有方法在性能-吞吐量权衡方面取得了更优的结果。

Conclusion: GrowPage表明将KV预算从静态预定义约束转变为按需动态资源配置，能够更有效地匹配推理工作负载中注意力需求的动态变化，从而在保持服务吞吐量的同时改善推理性能。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning has made the key--value (KV) cache a critical memory bottleneck for efficient LLM serving. Existing KV compression methods usually rely on a predefined per-request budget and adjust only which KV states are retained, leaving the total capacity fixed throughout decoding. However, reasoning workloads exhibit substantial demand variation: different requests require different KV capacities, and the attention demand of an individual request evolves during generation. We introduce \textbf{GrowPage}, an on-demand KV budgeting framework that treats KV capacity as a runtime resource. GrowPage maintains lightweight dual-timescale query summaries to capture recent and long-term attention behaviors, and uses their relative attention working sets to estimate demand evolution. At each capacity boundary, GrowPage either compresses KV states within the current allocation or acquires an additional physical page when broader demand emerges. By integrating with PagedAttention's page-level memory abstraction, GrowPage preserves continuous batching and prefix caching. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks across multiple models show that GrowPage achieves a superior performance--throughput trade-off over existing approaches.

</details>


### [131] [PPO-STGNN: A Proximal Policy Optimization Approach with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks for DAG Task Scheduling in Cloud-Edge-End Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03503)
*Yangshuo Qi,Chenwei Wang,Zihan Shen,Songlin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PPO-STGNN，一种结合近端策略优化（PPO）与时空图神经网络（STGNN）的DAG任务调度算法，用于云边端协同环境下的有向无环图任务调度，通过STGNN提取任务拓扑和物理资源图的时空特征，利用PPO优化调度策略以最小化makespan和调度长度比（SLR），并通过多教师行为克隆机制加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边端环境中计算能力、网络带宽和能耗高度异构，具有复杂依赖关系的DAG任务调度是NP难问题；传统启发式算法和常规强化学习方法难以捕捉系统资源的时空动态特性。

Method: 1) 使用STGNN同时提取DAG任务拓扑特征和云边端物理资源图的特征；2) 通过PPO算法优化调度策略，目标为最小化makespan和SLR，同时改善CPU和内存负载均衡；3) 引入多教师行为克隆机制进行预训练以加速收敛。

Result: 实验结果表明PPO-STGNN在保持低完成时间的同时显著提升了负载均衡性能，适用于动态异构的云边端DAG调度场景。

Conclusion: PPO-STGNN通过融合PPO与STGNN，能够有效捕获系统资源的时空动态信息，在异构云边端环境下实现高效的DAG任务调度，兼顾低延迟与良好的负载均衡。

Abstract: With the rapid development of the Internet of Things, computation intensive directed acyclic graph (DAG) tasks have become increasingly common in cloud-edge-end collaborative environments. However, cloud, edge, and end nodes are highly heterogeneous in computing capacity, network bandwidth, and energy consumption, which makes the efficient scheduling of tasks with complex dependencies an NP-hard problem. Traditional heuristic algorithms and conventional reinforcement-learning methods often fail to capture the spatio-temporal dynamics of system resources. This paper proposes PPO-STGNN, a DAG task-scheduling algorithm that integrates proximal policy optimization (PPO) with spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs). The method uses an STGNN to extract features from both the DAG task topology and the physical cloud-edge-end resource graph, and then optimizes the scheduling policy through PPO to minimize makespan and schedule length ratio (SLR) while improving CPU and memory load balancing. To accelerate convergence, a multi-teacher behavior-cloning mechanism is introduced for pretraining. Experimental results show that PPO-STGNN significantly improves load balancing while maintaining a low completion time, making it suitable for dynamic and heterogeneous cloud-edge- end DAG scheduling scenarios.

</details>


### [132] [What Matters for Aggressive Decoding-Time KV Eviction? Temporal Aggregation and Ranking Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03515)
*Bo Zeng,Yu Zhao,Yefeng Liu,Zhihong Lu,Xuanfan Ni,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究解码时KV缓存压缩中时间聚合规则（EMA聚合）的作用，发现EMA使近似保序的打分器在驱逐集层面难以区分，提出基于EMA的驱逐方法InertiaKV及其懒惰变体InertiaKV-Lazy，并引入Score-Free解码作为独立操作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩研究过度关注设计更好的token打分函数，而时间聚合规则（跨解码步聚合分数的规则）被视为实现细节。作者发现在激进压缩下，EMA聚合使得近似保序的打分器修改在驱逐集层面几乎无区别，表明时间聚合与排名保持是独立且重要的设计因素，但尚未被充分认识。

Method: 首先系统比较多种打分器（注意值、值范数、熵、KeyDiff、键范数、近期性、学习型打分器）在EMA聚合下的表现，揭示其相关性差异。基于观察引入InertiaKV（EMA解码时驱逐方法）和InertiaKV-Lazy（周期性刷新变体，取得1.34-1.46x解码吞吐）。另提出Score-Free解码：首次解码步对整个上下文打分一次后冻结排名并移除后续所有评分。

Result: 在六个开放权重主干模型以及LongBench、LongBench-v2、RULER基准上：InertiaKV-Lazy相对完整刷新InertiaKV取得1.34-1.46x解码吞吐提升；Score-Free解码在移除全部后续评分情况下平均质量变化仅为+0.03（即几乎无损失甚至略优）。实验辨识出时间聚合与排名保持是独立且影响显著的设计因素，同时说明打分质量在一般情况下并非无关紧要。

Conclusion: 时间聚合规则和排名保持是KV缓存压缩中与打分质量正交的关键设计维度，EMA聚合耦合了层加权与时间保持，为设计高效解码时驱逐方法提供了新思路；实验结果并不否认打分质量的重要性，而是将设计空间从单一的"打分函数"扩展为"打分+聚合+排名保持"的系统性视角。
